# Arbites: original English description comparison

[繁體中文](../LOCALIZATION_COMPARISON.md) | [Player descriptions](README.md) | [Technical index](SOURCE_INDEX.md)

Implementation: Release 1.13.1, fixed SHA `7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e`. English: Steam Build `25606770`, `content/localization/ui`, resource hash `63564edcb7c3c5ee`, language `en` (code `0`). English UI export SHA-256 `fb54bb69880e08b3a6e1c359bd970ed2416748bf5513d45c4b8a39c545e8c668`.

The local UI resource was extracted on 2026-10-02. Full source text remains in the Git-ignored version archive `source/SteamBuild_25606770_1.13.1/`. English and implementation sources both correspond to 1.13.1; runtime differences still require an in-game check.

Judge English meaning against existing fixed-version mechanism evidence. Missing details are supplementary; only explicit English contradictions receive a player erratum. Chinese verdicts are not inherited. Placeholder substitution is reconstruction rather than an observed game screen.

While active, general reduction also affects ranged damage, and ranged hits do not clear stacks. on_player_hit_received checks only attack_type = melee before finishing, with no damage > 0 or block-result check. Attack queues the event on the hit player buff extension; an ally being hit does not clear these stacks. [Fixed source](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/adamant_buff_templates.lua#L3418-L3448) [Fixed source](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/utilities/attack/attack.lua#L752-L784) [Fixed source](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L689-L727) [Fixed source](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/adamant_buff_templates.lua#L3403-L3417) | Not covered by the description | The English names the next consuming melee hit without explicitly excluding ranged protection before it; the event details supplement that wording. |

## Comparison totals

The 487 listed rules comprise **239 Consistent**, **1 Explicit contradiction**, **234 Not covered by the description**, **0 No corresponding implementation evidence found** and **13 Cannot confirm**. The explicit English contradiction concerns the melee attack-interval value in Fear of Justice. These totals apply only to the listed rules.
